# Invisible Ships — Journal Part 03 (29 Sep – 8 Nov 2025)

Entries and recordings.

This file joins 57 files from the Invisible Ships archive so it can be uploaded to an AI assistant in one piece. Each file starts with a line `===== FILE: <path> · ID: <id> =====` and keeps its metadata header; cite the ID. Nothing has been rewritten. The disclaimer below applies to every file in this bundle (it is repeated at the end of each file in the full archive). For how to use this with an AI, read bundle 01 or `00-START-HERE.md`.

> **Critical Disclaimer.** Transcripts and statements recorded in this archive are external communications and do NOT represent the author's beliefs, views, or intent. Nothing here accuses, blames, or alleges malfeasance by any named person, company, or government body, and all of this information requires independent verification. Full terms: `meta/IS_META_terms.md` and https://www.invisibleships.com/disclaimer.

**Monday September 29th transcripts summary:**

  - What are our children going through? Capturing information from a local missing person poster.
  - The author (myself) envisioning what assistance may look like and if any kind of physical and or emotional comfort is possible now and in the future.
  - Are the so-called “euthanization specialists” showing up in-person at the intersection of 8th Ave. & Colorado Blvd. and deciding not to euthanize me?

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene:** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

# Sep 29, 2025 — Recording 1 (13:46)

**Audio:** [20250929-134613.mp3](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1NpmfvdkCVy7aVKselrH4O78JJbCvaPh3/view?usp=sharing)

**1:48pm, reviewing information from a local missing person poster “Soleil Lund” in Denver, CO.*

File duration: 04:50

  
"I'm just thinking about euthanization victims." [00:03]  
"Uh, there's this organization, A Voice for the Voiceless, a missing poster." [00:13]  
"So Leo, S O L E I L Lund." [00:19]  
"L U N D." [00:21]  
"So Leon, 20 years old, has been missing since August 23rd, 2025." [00:29]  
"About 30 days now in Denver, Colorado." [00:34]  
"She is 5' 2 inches tall, 120 lbs with brown hair and blue eyes, and was last seen wearing red shorts with tassels." [00:45]  
"A gray long sleeve sweatshirt with a Hamsa hand design." [00:52]  
"Teva sandals." [00:54]  
"She does not have a phone, ID or belongings with her and has light pastel butterfly tattoos on her left shoulder along with piercings in her ears and belly button." [01:08]  
"So Leil had traveled to Colorado for treatment but was left without proper care and was last seen near the Colorado coalition for the homeless off Broadway and Stout Street." [01:22]  
"She has mental health concerns and her family is very worried for her safety." [01:27]  
"If you have any information, please call the Denver Police Department at 720-913-2800." [01:37]  
"Case number 25437312." [01:42]  
"I don't think I've seen you." [01:47]  
"Um this uh past suggestion that has been shared a few times." [01:57]  
"I hope our kids make it back from the concert." [02:00]  
"You know, this happens, this was happening before uh, this strange new world with citizen, the so-called citizen tech phenomenon of communication in it began." [02:20]  
"It's saddening." [02:26]  
"A voice for the voiceless,[ www](http://www)." [02:30]  
"AVFTV.com." [02:34]  
"It's 1:48 p.m., Monday, September 29th, 2025." [02:42]  
"End of recording." [02:45]  
"Well, would anyone care to say anything from the distance?" [02:51]  
"Repeating in 3, 2, 1." [02:57]  
"I cannot believe the reaction I am having to this recording." [03:11]  
"I think this person may have lost their life, Sean Christopher Harris." [03:27]  
"Yes, that is exactly what I said with 100% word-for-word accuracy." [03:43]  
"I am saying that because of this imagery." [03:54]  
"Don't leave without taking a picture of that." [04:02]  
"Telepathic visuals indicate POV video of possibly a taller male walking behind a person with a gray sweatshirt." [04:25]  
"Matching this girl's description." [04:33]  
"I wonder if there's any truth to that." [04:38]  
"End of recording." [04:47]

Questions & Comments:

  - While capturing this recording telepathic visual information was suggested that Soleil was euthanized from behind with an injection device by a taller caucasian male with short-medium length dark hair.
  - There is a pattern of suggestion that facilitators of this so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” will attempt to take credit for any information that helps them inflate their presence in an effort to overpower the victim into accepting euthanization.
  - Is the presence of this unconsented-to so-called “Citizen Tech” phenomena of communication exposing Denver CO.’s children and young adults to extraordinary danger, for example euthanization for communicating?
  - There is an old pattern of suggestion that my six year old daughter is dead already and if not she will be executed.

File duration: 28:56

**Wednesday October 1st transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - Have I and the unknown contributors arrived at a “technological awareness” milestone in the so-called “Citizen Tech” journey?. Also, “the hangman” makes an introduction and warns us that a noose is waiting for people participating in treason.
  - A brief capture at the bookstore continues to suggest that individuals who find out they are communicating with humans may be executed for not believing in the “spirituality narrative” 
  - Additional innocent citizens are added to the “euthanization list” at another “Battle for the Intersection”.
  -   

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

This entry also includes some manually captured transcripts using my laptop.

**Scene:** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Thursday October 2nd transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - Who holds the keys to the “Citizen Tech” conversation and possible has influence over the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” in Denver, CO.?
  - Are we all experiencing “a world of opposites”? Would aligning ourselves with the Japanese philosophy of “Kaizen” help us move forward in our journey without punishing each other?
  - Later at another “Battle for the Intersection”...

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene:** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Saturday October 11th transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - …
  - Later at another “Battle for the Intersection”...

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed. Later at another “Battle for the Intersection” I hold a cardboard sign while standing in the median.

Is today the day that a positive change will come to the city of Denver, CO.? There is also a pattern of suggestion that continuing to add transcripts to this document only delays support for myself and others.

I’m setting my bar for expectations extremely low. I am reluctant to continue capturing transcripts. It’s been about a week since I lost my bed at the 48th St. shelter for not making curfew. The last time this happened it took me about a week to make it back there and my productivity suffered during that time.

Regardless of if I lose my life attempting to surface the violence Denver’s citizens are experiencing, I choose to believe that I will make it back to my daughter and maybe these transcripts will help remove the threat of euthanization and usher in the consent missing from our “Citizen Tech” conversations so all of our children can communicate safely, in the future. *In-progress

**Sunday October 15th transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - Additional drivers are added to the so-called “euthanization list” at another “Battle for the Intersection”.

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Wednesday October 22nd transcripts summary:**

  - “Let me out!” attempting to capture the painful experiences of others contributing to the so-called “Citizen Tech” phenomena of communication including the suggestion of feedback from the facilitators of “ugly workflows” who are now experiencing the “breaching process” instead of just facilitating it.
  - Capturing my almost daily self-care practice
  - Continuing to surface the spread of the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” to passing vehicles at another “Battle for the Intersection”.
  - Overarching themes and takeaways from the intersection and the last 24hrs.

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Friday October 24th transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information including the suggestion of the horrible smell a victim’s body emanates during euthanization being experienced by the DRM (Denver Rescue Mission) 48th St. shelter, other residents there and individuals in other locations.
  - …

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Saturday October 25th summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before at the DRM (Denver Rescue Mission) 48th St. Shelter and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - Surfacing the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” at another “Battle for the Intersection”.
  - Denver’s law enforcement having taken control of the drug trade and that control being seen as a new type of decriminalization has become a pattern of suggestion
  - Homeland Security and or the DEA offering me an opportunity to sell drugs has become a pattern of suggestion
  - The FBI waiting for me to accept euthanization so they can see who shows up has become a pattern of suggestion
  - Capturing a threat to a family member with a military background 

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Sunday October 26th transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including more POV (Point of View) visual information
  - MIT (Massachusetts Institute of Technology) presence continues to be suggested in the so-called “Citizen Tech” conversation

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

**Wednesday November 5th transcripts summary:**

  - Denver’s citizens are bombarded with a new suggestion surfaces at today’s battle for the intersection (paraphrasing) “Do not Google that information or you will be punished!” as they continue to be added to the so-called “Euthanization List” for communicating. 
  - …

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.

##### **Manual entry**

**12:30 - 3:00pm approx. No recording available for this entry. My phone died before making it to the intersection in an attempt to surface the threat of the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows”.*

My individual suppression and the threat of the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” is still present at the intersection of 8th Ave. & Colorado Blvd. in Denver, Colorado. People continue to be brutalized with an un-consented “Citizen Tech” conversation including the suggestion “Do not support this person with the sign!” or “Do not support this person to the left of you!”. The “Citizen Tech” suggestion seems to emanate from the vehicle itself and if the vehicle responds, they are added to the “Euthanization List”.

The phenomena of sound at the intersection suggests that if you are within my presence and you are thinking or reading my sign for example, your thoughts are completely transparent and they can be heard at the intersection, by myself or anyone passing by. I often recommend in “Citizen Tech” conversation that people hold their ears shut with their index fingers to test for a change in the volume of the conversation. If there is an increase in the volume of the “Citizen Tech” conversation, your ears are in use. Strangely, that is both the suggestion and the human experience for myself and at least one other brave human being that approached me at the DRM (Denver Rescue Mission) 48th St. Shelter asking me about the strange conversation in the building and using the word “acoustics”.

Historically, 30-50 vehicles per week conservatively speaking are added to the euthanization list from the same location. Imagine a six lane thoroughfare. Three lanes on each side. I’m standing in the middle on the median facing the southbound drivers side window with vehicles backing up for several hundred feet at each stop light. Anytime I look in the direction of a vehicle the unsolicited “Citizen Tech” communication begins “Do not support this person, to the left of you!”. Unfortunately there is no way of avoiding looking at the people driving by and for every three to four stops a “Citizen Tech” comment would emanate from a specific vehicle. For example, “Why are you saying this to me?” or “Don’t worry. I wont.”.

The sign I use at the intersection to find donations surfaces information from the unknown facilitators of the so-called “Citizen Tech” conversation. For example, previously I used a sign that read “Free Palestine” and it surfaced a pattern of communication i.e. (paraphrasing) “His sign says free Palestine. I don’t feel good about doing this to this person”. Is this response evidence of the presence of Palestinian communicators?

Today I tried a new sign that read “Google The Battle for Your Brain. Anything Helps.” and it immediately surfaced deeply concerning change in the historical suggestion to the vehicles, breaking a daily pattern of communication spanning over 1 year now. In addition to  “Do not support this person!” the facilitators of the so-called “ugly workflows” continued with “Do not Google that information!” and even worse “You will be punished if you Google that information!” many times over. The energy of the conversation with the vehicles was angry and the delivery was relentless minute to minute over a period of two to three hours.

Tragically, the telepathic “Citizen Tech” reaction to the sign from the people driving by suggests a peak in responses from the vehicles and in turn unfortunate additions to the so-called euthanization “ugly workflows” for simply reading the sign. 

Remember, reading or thinking within the range of this so-called “Citizen Technology” will share your thoughts publicly with anyone near you. During my repeating process at the intersection when I am recording every word suggested to the vehicles an open car window may reveal this phenomena with the driver. Occasionally, I will capture the suggestion of a response from the driver (paraphrasing) “How is that possible? How did they know what I was thinking?”. Can you imagine the surprise of both people at that moment?

Unfortunately there is no public explanation for what is happening to the residents and visitors in Denver, Colorado. Without explanation of this phenomena and during what has become a long season of protesting in America, is this city of Denver, Colorado sitting on a “hot bed”?

  

How vulnerable is America at this moment? How long will the government shutdown last? *Include Microsoft protest, ICE protests… *In-progress

**Saturday November 8th transcripts summary:**

  - Themes from the last 24hrs, takeaways from the night before and early morning including the suggestion of the citizens ability to silence the life threatening so-called “Citizen Tech” conversation demonstrated initially at the DRM (Denver Rescue Mission) 48th St. Shelter in Denver, CO..  
  - At another so-called “Battle for the Intersection” Denver Colorado’s citizens experience the third day in a row where their vehicles are bombarded with a new threatening statement “Do not Google that information!” breaking a historical pattern lasting over one year.

**Methodology:** I capture manual transcripts of the telepathic and or audible “Citizen Tech” phenomena by repeating the suggestions I receive out loud while recording using the native Android voice recorder. Transcriptions of the .mp3 recordings are generated with Google AI Studio. [**See sample screen recording*](https://drive.google.com/file/d/16PkRWIlH4c6SZxpFRagmVGgMt12eE8tG/view?usp=sharing)

**Scene(s):** I’m traveling around downtown Denver, Colorado by scooter and on foot capturing recordings as needed.
